4.6. Conclusions


EbA has the potential to play a key role in helping         The recommendations we have laid out here are
society adapt to climate change and putting the             intended to push EbA to the next level, and to harness
world on a more climate-resilient and nature-positive       the power of nature to help people adapt to climate
pathway. While EbA may not be applicable in every           change at a pace and scale that is commensurate with
instance, it has the potential to meet many of society’s    the magnitude of the climate challenge. We recognize
diverse adaptation needs and priorities, while also         that these recommendations are ambitious and that
conserving and restoring the ecosystems on which            their implementation will require tremendous effort,
our world depends. It can also help the world meet          political will, and significant financial and human
multiple global objectives including not only climate       resources. Scaling up will take time and success is
change adaptation, but also climate change mitigation,      not guaranteed. However, inaction is not an option
biodiversity conservation and sustainable development       – the world cannot afford to wait. Without rapid and
more broadly.                                               significant adaptation action, climate change will
                                                            have increasingly devastating impacts on human
However, in order to harness the full potential of EbA,     communities, natural ecosystems and economies
it will be important to significantly increase the pace     worldwide. Ambitious and rapid action on EbA is
and scale of EbA implementation across the world.           needed on all fronts and by all stakeholders if we are to
There is a need for much greater implementation of          accelerate the transition towards a climate-resilient and
EbA, backed by supportive policies and regulations and      nature-positive pathway that is better for both people
significant financial resources. There are significant      and nature.
opportunities both to ramp up the number of EbA
initiatives globally and to implement larger, longer-term
and more impactful initiatives in support of climate
adaptation goals.
